## Comprehensive Project Exploration Report: DuckDB Data Agent

I've thoroughly explored the DuckDB Data Agent project. Here's a detailed breakdown:

### 1. OVERALL PROJECT STRUCTURE

**Frontend/Backend Separation:** Clearly separated
- **Frontend:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/` - React 18 + TypeScript SPA
- **Backend:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/` - FastAPI Python server
- **Deployment:** Single Docker image (multi-stage build) that serves frontend from backend's `/static` directory

**Project Layout:**
```
├── frontend/                    # React 18 + TypeScript SPA
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── components/          # 20 component files (AgentPanel, MessageBubble, etc.)
│   │   ├── agent/               # agentService.ts - SSE event handling
│   │   ├── AgentContext.tsx     # Agent state management & streaming logic
│   │   ├── types.ts             # Shared TypeScript interfaces
│   │   ├── App.tsx              # Main app with mode toggle
│   │   ├── main.tsx             # React entry point
│   │   └── *.css               # Component styles
│   ├── vite.config.ts           # Vite dev server with /api proxy to :8000
│   └── package.json
├── backend/                     # FastAPI + DuckDB + Claude Agent SDK
│   ├── app/
│   │   ├── main.py              # FastAPI app setup & CORS
│   │   ├── config.py            # Environment config (API key, model)
│   │   ├── agent.py             # Agent loop & SSE streaming (197 lines)
│   │   ├── database.py          # DuckDB connection & query execution
│   │   ├── tools.py             # Agent SDK tool definitions
│   │   ├── routes/
│   │   │   ├── chat.py          # POST /api/chat - SSE streaming
│   │   │   ├── query.py         # POST /api/query - SQL execution
│   │   │   └── tables.py        # GET/POST/DELETE /api/tables
│   │   └── data/                # Sample datasets (titanic.csv)
│   ├── pyproject.toml           # Poetry dependencies
│   └── .env / .env.example      # Configuration
├── Dockerfile                   # Multi-stage build (Node + Python)
└── Makefile                     # Dev commands
```

---

### 2. BACKEND DEEP DIVE

**Framework:** FastAPI (async Python)

**Key Dependencies** (from pyproject.toml):
```
python = "^3.12"
fastapi = "^0.129.0"
uvicorn = "^0.41.0" (with standard extras)
duckdb = "^1.4.4"
python-dotenv = "^1.2.1"
python-multipart = "^0.0.22"
claude-agent-sdk = "^0.1.38"  # ← Claude Agent SDK for LLM calls
```

**How Agent Mode Works:**

1. **SSE Streaming (Server-Sent Events)**
   - Client sends POST `/api/chat` with user message
   - Backend streams response via `StreamingResponse` with `text/event-stream` MIME type
   - Frontend uses `fetch()` with `response.body.getReader()` to consume stream

2. **Agent Loop Implementation** (`backend/app/agent.py`, lines 62-196):
   ```python
   async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
       duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()  # Create MCP server with execute_sql tool
       options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
           model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,  # Default: "claude-opus-4-6" (can be set via env)
           system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),  # Dynamic - includes table schema
           mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},  # DuckDB MCP server
           allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
           permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
           max_turns=20,
           include_partial_messages=True,
       )
       client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
       await client.connect()
       await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
       
       # Stream events: thinking_delta, text_delta, tool_use, tool_result, done
   ```

3. **Event Types Streamed**:
   - `thinking` - Extended thinking blocks (token by token)
   - `thinking_done` - Signals end of thinking, start of answer
   - `tool_call` - SQL query being executed (includes id, name, sql)
   - `tool_result` - Query results (columns, rows, rowCount)
   - `answer` - Text response from assistant
   - `error` - Agent errors
   - `done` - Stream complete with session_id

4. **Agent Tools** (`backend/app/tools.py`):
   - Single MCP tool: `execute_sql`
   - Uses Claude Agent SDK's `@tool` decorator
   - Creates MCP server via `create_sdk_mcp_server()`
   - Executes DuckDB queries and returns JSON with columns/rows/rowCount
   - Max 100 rows returned, but full rowCount included

5. **Database Layer** (`backend/app/database.py`):
   - Singleton `Database` class with in-memory DuckDB connection
   - Methods: `execute_query()`, `load_csv()`, `get_table_info()`, `list_tables()`, `drop_table()`
   - Uses DuckDB's `read_csv_auto()` for automatic schema detection

**API Routes:**
- `GET /api/health` - Health check
- `GET /api/tables` - List all tables with schema
- `POST /api/query` - Execute SQL (returns `{columns, rows, rowCount, resultType}`)
- `POST /api/upload` - Upload CSV file (multipart/form-data)
- `POST /api/upload/sample` - Load Titanic dataset
- `DELETE /api/tables/{table_name}` - Drop table
- `POST /api/chat` - **SSE stream of agent responses**

---

### 3. FRONTEND DEEP DIVE

**Framework:** React 18 + TypeScript + Vite

**Key Dependencies** (from package.json):
```json
"react": "^18.3.1",
"react-dom": "^18.3.1",
"react-markdown": "^10.1.0",
"remark-gfm": "^4.0.1"
```

**Two UI Modes:**

#### **Editor Mode** (Default)
Located in `App.tsx` lines 134-162:
- Header: "DuckDB Data Agent" title + "Agent Mode" button (styled with indigo)
- Mode header: "Editor Mode" label
- Main content:
  - `FileUpload` - Drag-drop CSV or sample data button
  - `QueryEditor` - Text area with Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to execute
  - `ResultsTable` - Interactive table with sorting/filtering
  - `ResultMarkdown` - For EXPLAIN query results

#### **Agent Mode** (When toggled)
Located in `App.tsx` lines 120-132:
- Header: "DuckDB Data Agent" title + "Editor Mode" button (styled with indigo, active state)
- Content area replaced with `<AgentPanel />`

**Agent Mode UI Components:**

1. **AgentPanel** (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx`):
   ```
   .agent-panel
   ├── .agent-panel__header
   │   ├── "Agent Mode" title (small gray text)
   │   └── "Clear" button (appears if messages exist)
   ├── .agent-panel__messages (scrollable)
   │   └── MessageBubble (repeated for each message)
   └── ChatInput
   ```
   - Auto-scrolls to bottom when new messages arrive
   - "Clear" button resets conversation and aborts streaming

2. **ChatInput** (`frontend/src/components/ChatInput.tsx`):
   - Textarea with auto-height (up to 150px)
   - "Send" button
   - Enter to send, Shift+Enter for newline
   - Placeholder changes based on streaming state

3. **MessageBubble** (`frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx`):
   - User message: Light indigo background (`#eef2ff`), left-aligned
   - Assistant message: White with border (`#e5e7eb`), right-aligned
   - **Segments** structure for structured thinking/tool/answer:
     ```
     ContentSegment {
       type: 'thinking' | 'tool' | 'answer'
       text?: string
       toolResult?: ToolCallResult
     }
     ```
   - **Thinking Block** (collapsible `<details>`):
     - Shows last line of thinking as preview
     - Expands to show full thinking + tool results
     - Collapses automatically when answer phase starts
   - **Answer Segments** - Green left border, labeled "Answer"
   - Uses `react-markdown` with GitHub-flavored markdown support

4. **InlineQueryResult** (`frontend/src/components/InlineQueryResult.tsx`):
   - Displays tool execution results inline
   - **For SQL tools** (execute_sql):
     - Green label: "SQL Query"
     - Shows SQL code in gray box
     - Renders table with columns/rows (max 20 rows displayed)
     - Shows row count and truncation notice
   - **For other tools**:
     - Different colored labels (bash=purple, generic=blue)
     - Shows input (command/input) and text output
   - **Error state** - Red border, error text displayed
   - **Pending state** - "Executing..." status

**Styling Notes:**
- Color palette: Indigo (#6366f1), grays (#1f2937, #9ca3af), greens (for answers), reds (for errors)
- Responsive grid layout: sidebar (250px fixed, collapsible) + main content
- "App Mode" header is sticky at top
- Agent panel fills remaining space with flex layout

### 5. BACKEND STREAMING & FRONTEND COMMUNICATION

**Streaming Protocol: Server-Sent Events (SSE)**

**Backend Stream Generation** (`agent.py`, lines 91-189):
```python
async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    # Parse message from Claude SDK
    # Emit different SSE events based on message type:
    
    if event_type == "content_block_delta":
        delta_type = delta.get("type")
        if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
        elif delta_type == "text_delta":
            yield f"event: answer\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    
    elif event_type == "content_block_start":
        if block_type == "thinking":
            has_thinking = True
        elif block_type == "text":
            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
    
    # For ToolUseBlock: emit tool_call with id, name, sql
    # Execute SQL immediately and emit tool_result
```

**Frontend Event Consumption** (`agentService.ts`, lines 14-72):
```typescript
export async function runAgentLoop(
  message: string,
  sessionId: string | null,
  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<void> {
  const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    signal,
  });
  
  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = '';
  
  while (true) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    
    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    
    // Parse event: thinking, answer, tool_call, tool_result, done, error
    // Call corresponding callbacks
  }
}
```

**AgentContext State Management** (`AgentContext.tsx`):
- Buffers text chunks and flushes every 50ms (batching optimization)
- Segments approach: Accumulates thinking/tool/answer segments
- `ContentSegment[]` tracks structured conversation blocks
- Session ID persistence for multi-turn conversations
- Abort controller for cancellation support

**Event Flow:**
1. User sends message → `fetch('/api/chat', { body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id }) })`
2. Backend streams SSE events
3. Frontend parses line-by-line (SSE format: `event: <type>\ndata: <json>\n\n`)
4. Callbacks update React state with segments
5. MessageBubble re-renders with thinking (collapsible) + tool results + answer

### 6. OBSERVABILITY & TRACING

**Current Setup (Minimal):**

**Backend Logging:**
- `logging` module imported in `agent.py`
- `logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)`
- Used for:
  - `logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)` - Model selection logging
  - `logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)` - Message parse errors
- No formal tracing/instrumentation setup
- No Sentry, Datadog, or other external observability tools

**Frontend Observability:**
- No logging framework
- `console.error()` used for debugging only
- No analytics or telemetry

**Missing Elements:**
- No request tracing/correlation IDs
- No performance metrics (except client-side `performance.now()` for SQL execution time)
- No error tracking service
- No structured logging (using Python logging basics only)
- No OpenTelemetry instrumentation
- No agent step-by-step execution metrics

## Langfuse Python SDK -- Complete Integration Research

### 1. Installation and Basic Setup

**Install the package:**

```bash
pip install langfuse
```

For Anthropic integration, also install:

```bash
pip install opentelemetry-instrumentation-anthropic
```

**Environment variables** (all three are required for Langfuse to function):

```bash
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY="sk-lf-..."
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY="pk-lf-..."
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL="https://cloud.langfuse.com"       # EU region (default)
# LANGFUSE_BASE_URL="https://us.cloud.langfuse.com"  # US region
```

Note: In v3, the environment variable is `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` (not `LANGFUSE_HOST` as in v2).

**Client initialization** uses a singleton pattern via `get_client()`:

```python
from langfuse import get_client

langfuse = get_client()
```

Or via the constructor with explicit credentials:

```python
from langfuse import Langfuse

langfuse = Langfuse(
    public_key="pk-lf-...",
    secret_key="sk-lf-...",
    base_url="https://cloud.langfuse.com",
)
```

---

### 2. Checking if Langfuse is Properly Configured/Connected

Use `auth_check()` to verify the connection:

```python
from langfuse import get_client

langfuse = get_client()

if langfuse.auth_check():
    print("Langfuse client is authenticated and ready!")
else:
    print("Authentication failed. Check your credentials and host.")
```

For debug logging:

```python
import logging
langfuse_logger = logging.getLogger("langfuse")
langfuse_logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

# OR during initialization:
langfuse = Langfuse(debug=True)
```

---

### 3. Creating Traces with Spans and Generations (Context Manager Approach)

The v3 SDK provides `start_as_current_observation()` as a context manager. The `as_type` parameter controls the observation type: `"span"` (default) or `"generation"` (for LLM calls).

```python
from langfuse import get_client, propagate_attributes

langfuse = get_client()

with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
    as_type="span",
    name="user-request-pipeline",
    input={"user_query": "Tell me a joke"},
) as root_span:
    with propagate_attributes(user_id="user_123", session_id="session_abc"):
        # Nested generation for an LLM call
        with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
            as_type="generation",
            name="joke-generation",
            model="claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
        ) as generation:
            # ... make LLM call ...
            generation.update(output="Why did the chicken cross the road?")

    root_span.update(output={"final_joke": "..."})
```

---

### 4. Decorator-Based Approach (`@observe`)

The `@observe()` decorator is the simplest way to instrument code. The outermost decorated function automatically becomes a **trace**, and inner decorated functions become **spans**. Use `as_type="generation"` for LLM calls.

```python
from langfuse import observe, get_client, propagate_attributes

@observe()
def main_pipeline(user_query: str):
    """This becomes a trace (outermost decorated function)."""
    langfuse = get_client()

    with propagate_attributes(
        user_id="user_123",
        session_id="session_abc",
        metadata={"pipeline": "main"},
    ):
        result = process_query(user_query)
        langfuse.update_current_trace(
            input={"question": user_query},
            output={"answer": result},
        )
        return result

@observe()
def process_query(query: str):
    """This becomes a span (inner decorated function)."""
    return call_llm(query)

@observe(name="llm-call", as_type="generation")
def call_llm(prompt: str):
    """This becomes a generation observation."""
    # ... make LLM API call ...
    return "LLM response text"
```

Key features of `@observe()`:
- Automatically captures: function name, arguments, return value, exceptions, execution time
- Nesting is maintained automatically via Python's `contextvars`
- Works with both sync and async functions
- You can disable I/O capture for performance: `@observe(capture_input=False, capture_output=False)`

---

### 5. Tracing Anthropic Claude API Calls Specifically

There are two approaches:

**Approach A: OpenTelemetry Instrumentation (recommended)**

```python
import os
from langfuse import get_client
from opentelemetry.instrumentation.anthropic import AnthropicInstrumentor

# 1. Initialize Langfuse
langfuse = get_client()

# 2. Instrument the Anthropic SDK (auto-traces all Anthropic calls)
AnthropicInstrumentor().instrument()

# 3. Use Anthropic normally -- calls are traced automatically
from anthropic import Anthropic

client = Anthropic()

message = client.messages.create(
    model="claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
    max_tokens=1000,
    system="You are a helpful assistant.",
    messages=[
        {"role": "user", "content": "Why is the ocean salty?"}
    ],
)
print(message.content)
```

**Approach B: Combined with `@observe` for richer context**

```python
from langfuse import observe, get_client, propagate_attributes
from opentelemetry.instrumentation.anthropic import AnthropicInstrumentor
from anthropic import Anthropic

AnthropicInstrumentor().instrument()

@observe()
def my_llm_pipeline(user_input: str):
    langfuse = get_client()

    with propagate_attributes(
        user_id="user_123",
        session_id="session_abc",
        tags=["agent"],
        metadata={"email": "user@example.com"},
    ):
        result = call_claude(user_input)
        langfuse.update_current_trace(input=user_input, output=result)
        return result

@observe(name="claude-call", as_type="generation")
def call_claude(prompt: str):
    client = Anthropic()
    message = client.messages.create(
        model="claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
        max_tokens=1024,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
    )
    return message.content[0].text
```

---

### 6. Creating Nested Spans (Trace -> Generation -> Tool Execution)

Here is a comprehensive example showing deeply nested observations:

```python
from langfuse import get_client, observe, propagate_attributes

langfuse = get_client()

# Using context managers for full control:
with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
    as_type="span", name="agent-run"
) as root_span:
    with propagate_attributes(user_id="user_123", session_id="sess_456"):

        # LLM generation (nested under root span)
        with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
            as_type="generation",
            name="claude-generation",
            model="claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
        ) as generation:
            generation.update(
                input=[{"role": "user", "content": "What's the weather?"}],
                output="I'll check the weather for you.",
                usage_details={"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 15},
            )

        # Tool execution span (nested under root span)
        with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
            as_type="span", name="tool-execution"
        ) as tool_span:
            tool_span.update(
                input={"tool": "get_weather", "args": {"city": "Paris"}},
                output={"temperature": "15C", "condition": "cloudy"},
            )

        # Follow-up generation after tool result
        with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
            as_type="generation",
            name="claude-final-response",
            model="claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
        ) as gen2:
            gen2.update(output="The weather in Paris is 15C and cloudy.")

    root_span.update(output="Completed agent run")
```

Or equivalently using the decorator approach:

```python
from langfuse import observe, get_client

@observe()
def agent_run(user_query: str):
    llm_response = call_llm(user_query)
    if needs_tool(llm_response):
        tool_result = execute_tool(llm_response)
        final = call_llm_with_tool_result(user_query, tool_result)
        return final
    return llm_response

@observe(name="llm-call", as_type="generation")
def call_llm(prompt: str):
    # ... Anthropic API call ...
    return "I'll check the weather."

@observe(name="tool-execution")
def execute_tool(llm_response: str):
    # ... run tool ...
    return {"temperature": "15C"}

@observe(name="llm-final", as_type="generation")
def call_llm_with_tool_result(prompt: str, tool_result: dict):
    # ... Anthropic API call with tool result ...
    return "The weather is 15C."
```

You can also use manual observations for parallel or fire-and-forget tasks:

```python
langfuse = get_client()

span = langfuse.start_observation(name="manual-span")
span.update(input="Data for side task")
child = span.start_observation(name="child-span", as_type="generation")
child.end()
span.end()
```

---

### 7. Environment Variables Summary

| Variable | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY` | Yes | Your project's public key (`pk-lf-...`) |
| `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY` | Yes | Your project's secret key (`sk-lf-...`) |
| `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` | Yes (has default) | API endpoint. Defaults to `https://cloud.langfuse.com` (EU). Use `https://us.cloud.langfuse.com` for US. For self-hosted, use your own URL. |
| `LANGFUSE_DEBUG` | No | Enable debug logging |
| `LANGFUSE_SAMPLE_RATE` | No | Sampling rate, e.g., `"0.2"` for 20% |
| `LANGFUSE_OBSERVE_DECORATOR_IO_CAPTURE_ENABLED` | No | Disable I/O capture globally for `@observe` |

**Important v2 -> v3 change**: The environment variable was renamed from `LANGFUSE_HOST` to `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` in SDK v3.

---

### 8. Getting the Langfuse Dashboard URL Programmatically

```python
from langfuse import get_client, observe

langfuse = get_client()

# Inside a context manager:
with langfuse.start_as_current_observation(as_type="span", name="my-task") as span:
    trace_url = langfuse.get_trace_url()
    print(f"View trace at: {trace_url}")

    # Or get the trace ID and construct/retrieve URL separately:
    trace_id = langfuse.get_current_trace_id()
    trace_url_by_id = langfuse.get_trace_url(trace_id=trace_id)

# Inside a decorated function:
@observe()
def my_function():
    langfuse = get_client()
    trace_url = langfuse.get_trace_url()
    trace_id = langfuse.get_current_trace_id()
    observation_id = langfuse.get_current_observation_id()
    print(f"Trace: {trace_url}")
```

You can also construct the URL manually if needed:

```python
base_url = os.environ.get("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
trace_id = langfuse.get_current_trace_id()
dashboard_url = f"{base_url}/trace/{trace_id}"
```

---

### 9. Flushing and Shutdown

For short-lived processes (scripts, serverless functions), always flush:

```python
langfuse = get_client()
# ... create traces ...
langfuse.flush()  # Send buffered data

# For graceful shutdown:
langfuse.shutdown()  # Flushes and terminates background threads
```

---

### Key SDK v3 Import Summary

```python
# Core client
from langfuse import get_client, Langfuse

# Decorator
from langfuse import observe

# Context propagation
from langfuse import propagate_attributes
```

This replaces the v2 imports which were:
```python
# v2 (deprecated)
from langfuse.decorators import langfuse_context, observe
```

> AGENT

Here are three approaches for integrating Langfuse:

---

## Approach 1: `@observe` decorator + manual spans (Recommended)

**How it works:**
- Wrap `stream_chat()` with `@observe()` — creates a trace per agent turn
- Inside the SSE streaming loop, create manual child spans for tool executions (SQL queries) and LLM generations
- Add a `/api/langfuse/status` endpoint that returns `{ enabled: bool, dashboardUrl: string }` so the frontend knows whether to show the button and where to link
- Langfuse is **optional** — if env vars are missing, tracing is silently skipped

**Pros:**
- Clean, Pythonic, minimal code changes to existing `agent.py`
- Full control over span structure (trace → generation → tool spans)
- Graceful degradation when Langfuse is not configured

**Cons:**
- Manual span management for each tool call in the streaming loop
- Need to handle async properly (Langfuse v3 supports async)

---

## Approach 2: OpenTelemetry auto-instrumentation

**How it works:**
- Install `opentelemetry-instrumentation-anthropic`
- It auto-instruments all Anthropic SDK calls made under the hood by `claude-agent-sdk`
- Minimal code changes — just initialize the instrumentor at app startup

**Pros:**
- Near-zero code changes
- Automatic capture of all LLM calls

**Cons:**
- `claude-agent-sdk` wraps the Anthropic SDK internally — auto-instrumentation may not capture calls correctly or may produce confusing trace structures
- Less control over trace naming, span hierarchy, and metadata
- Can't easily attach tool execution details (SQL queries, results) as separate spans

---

## Approach 3: Langfuse callback handler / middleware

**How it works:**
- Create a FastAPI middleware that wraps each `/api/chat` request in a Langfuse trace
- Inside `stream_chat()`, use context managers to create spans on-the-fly

**Pros:**
- Separation of concerns — tracing logic is outside business logic
- Works well for request-level tracing

**Cons:**
- Context propagation from middleware into async generators is tricky
- Still needs manual spans for tool calls, same as Approach 1 but with added middleware complexity

---

**My recommendation: Approach 1** — it gives us full control over the trace structure, works reliably with the Claude Agent SDK's streaming pattern, and keeps tracing logic co-located with the agent code where it belongs.

    62→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:

   113→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   114→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   115→                        current_text += text
   116→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   117→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   118→
   119→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   120→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   121→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   122→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   123→                        has_thinking = True
   124→                    elif block_type == "text":
   125→                        if has_thinking:
   126→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   127→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   128→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   129→                            thinking_sent = True
   130→                        has_tool_calls = True
   131→
   132→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   133→                if not actual_session_id:
   134→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   135→
   136→                for block in msg.content:
   137→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   138→                        has_tool_calls = True
   139→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   140→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   141→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   142→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   143→
   144→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   145→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   146→                        if sql:
   147→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   148→                        if command:
   149→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   150→                        if not sql and not command:
   151→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   152→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   153→
   154→                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results
   155→                        if sql:
   156→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   157→                            try:
   158→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   159→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   160→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   161→                            except Exception as e:
   162→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   163→
   164→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   165→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   166→                content = msg.content
   167→                if isinstance(content, list):
   168→                    for block in content:
   169→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   170→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   171→                                continue
   172→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   173→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   174→                            result_data: dict = {
   175→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   176→                                "name": name,
   177→                                "output": output,
   178→                            }
   179→                            if block.is_error:
   180→                                result_data["error"] = output
   181→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   182→
   183→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   184→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   185→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   186→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   187→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   188→                break
   189→
   190→    except Exception as e:
   191→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   192→    finally:
   193→        try:
   194→            await client.disconnect()
   195→        except Exception:
   196→            pass
   197→
```

     1→import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { AgentProvider } from './AgentContext';
     3→import { FileUpload } from './components/FileUpload';
     4→import { QueryEditor } from './components/QueryEditor';
     5→import { ResultsTable } from './components/ResultsTable';
     6→import { ResultMarkdown } from './components/ResultMarkdown';
     7→import { Sidebar } from './components/Sidebar';
     8→import { ErrorMessage } from './components/ErrorMessage';
     9→import { AgentPanel } from './components/AgentPanel';
    10→import type { TableInfo, QueryResult } from './types';
    11→import './App.css';
    12→
    13→function AppContent({ tables, refreshTables }: { tables: TableInfo[]; refreshTables: () => Promise<void> }) {
    14→  const [queryResult, setQueryResult] = useState<QueryResult | null>(null);
    15→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    16→  const [editorQuery, setEditorQuery] = useState<string | undefined>(
    17→    () => tables.length > 0 ? `SELECT * FROM "${tables[0].name}" LIMIT 100` : undefined
    18→  );
    19→  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
    20→  const [agentOpen, setAgentOpen] = useState(false);
    21→
    22→  const handleAgentToggle = () => {
    23→    setAgentOpen((prev) => !prev);
    24→  };
    25→
    26→  const handleLoadSample = useCallback(async () => {
    27→    setError(null);
    28→    try {
    29→      const response = await fetch('/api/upload/sample', { method: 'POST' });
    30→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load sample dataset');
    31→      await refreshTables();
    32→      setEditorQuery('SELECT * FROM "titanic" LIMIT 100');
    33→    } catch (e) {
    34→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load sample dataset');
    35→    }
    36→  }, [refreshTables]);
    37→
    38→  const handleFileUpload = useCallback(
    39→    async (file: File) => {
    40→      setError(null);
    41→      try {
    42→        const formData = new FormData();
    43→        formData.append('file', file);
    44→        const response = await fetch('/api/upload', {
    45→          method: 'POST',
    46→          body: formData,
    47→        });
    48→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to upload file');
    49→        const result = await response.json();
    50→        await refreshTables();
    51→        setEditorQuery(`SELECT * FROM "${result.name}" LIMIT 100`);
    52→      } catch (e) {
    53→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to upload file');
    54→      }
    55→    },
    56→    [refreshTables]
    57→  );
    58→
    59→  const handleQueryExecute = useCallback(
    60→    async (sql: string) => {
    61→      setError(null);
    62→      setQueryResult(null);
    63→      try {
    64→        const start = performance.now();
    65→        const response = await fetch('/api/query', {
    66→          method: 'POST',
    67→          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    68→          body: JSON.stringify({ sql }),
    69→        });
    70→        const elapsed = performance.now() - start;
    71→
    72→        if (!response.ok) {
    73→          const errorData = await response.json();
    74→          throw new Error(errorData.detail || 'Query execution failed');
    75→        }
    76→
    77→        const result = await response.json();
    78→        setQueryResult({
    79→          columns: result.columns,
    80→          rows: result.rows,
    81→          rowCount: result.rowCount,
    82→          executionTimeMs: elapsed,
    83→          resultType: result.resultType,
    84→        });
    85→        await refreshTables();
    86→      } catch (e) {
    87→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Query execution failed');
    88→      }
    89→    },
    90→    [refreshTables]
    91→  );
    92→
    93→  const handleTableClick = useCallback((tableName: string) => {
    94→    setEditorQuery(`SELECT * FROM "${tableName}" LIMIT 100`);
    95→  }, []);
    96→
    97→  const handleTableDelete = useCallback(async (tableName: string) => {
    98→    if (!confirm(`Delete table "${tableName}"?`)) return;
    99→    try {
   100→      const response = await fetch(`/api/tables/${encodeURIComponent(tableName)}`, {
   101→        method: 'DELETE',
   102→      });
   103→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete table');
   104→      await refreshTables();
   105→    } catch (e) {
   106→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to delete table');
   107→    }
   108→  }, [refreshTables]);
   109→
   110→  const appClass = [
   111→    'app',
   112→    sidebarCollapsed ? 'app--sidebar-collapsed' : '',
   113→  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   114→
   115→  return (
   116→    <div className={appClass}>
   117→      <div className="app__sidebar-wrapper">
   118→        <Sidebar tables={tables} onTableClick={handleTableClick} onTableDelete={handleTableDelete} collapsed={sidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setSidebarCollapsed((prev) => !prev)} />
   119→      </div>
   120→      {agentOpen ? (
   121→        <div className="app__agent-wrapper">
   122→          <div className="app__header">
   123→            <h1 className="app__title">DuckDB Data Agent</h1>
   124→            <button
   125→              className="app__agent-toggle app__agent-toggle--active"
   126→              onClick={handleAgentToggle}
   127→            >
   128→              Editor Mode
   129→            </button>
   130→          </div>
   131→          <AgentPanel />
   132→        </div>
   133→      ) : (
   134→        <div className="app__editor-wrapper">
   135→          <div className="app__header">
   136→            <h1 className="app__title">DuckDB Data Agent</h1>
   137→            <button
   138→              className="app__agent-toggle"
   139→              onClick={handleAgentToggle}
   140→            >
   141→              Agent Mode
   142→            </button>
   143→          </div>
   144→          <div className="app__mode-header">
   145→            <span className="app__mode-title">Editor Mode</span>
   146→          </div>
   147→          <main className="app__main">
   148→            <FileUpload onUpload={handleFileUpload} onLoadSample={handleLoadSample} />
   149→            <QueryEditor
   150→              onExecute={handleQueryExecute}
   151→              initialQuery={editorQuery}
   152→            />
   153→            {error && (
   154→              <ErrorMessage message={error} onDismiss={() => setError(null)} />
   155→            )}
   156→            {queryResult?.resultType === 'markdown' ? (
   157→              <ResultMarkdown result={queryResult} />
   158→            ) : (
   159→              <ResultsTable result={queryResult} />
   160→            )}
   161→          </main>
   162→        </div>
   163→      )}
   164→    </div>
   165→  );
   166→}
   167→
   168→export default function App() {
   169→  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);
   170→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   171→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   172→
   173→  const refreshTables = useCallback(async () => {
   174→    try {
   175→      const response = await fetch('/api/tables');
   176→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch tables');
   177→      const data = await response.json();
   178→      setTables(data);
   179→    } catch (e) {
   180→      console.error('Failed to refresh tables:', e);
   181→    }
   182→  }, []);
   183→
   184→  useEffect(() => {
   185→    (async () => {
   186→      try {
   187→        const response = await fetch('/api/health');
   188→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Backend is not available');
   189→        await refreshTables();
   190→      } catch (e) {
   191→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to connect to backend');
   192→      } finally {
   193→        setLoading(false);
   194→      }
   195→    })();
   196→  }, [refreshTables]);
   197→
   198→  if (loading) {
   199→    return <div className="app-loading">Connecting to backend...</div>;
   200→  }
   201→
   202→  if (error) {
   203→    return (
   204→      <div className="app-error">Failed to connect: {error}</div>
   205→    );
   206→  }
   207→
   208→  return (
   209→    <AgentProvider tables={tables} refreshTables={refreshTables}>
   210→      <AppContent tables={tables} refreshTables={refreshTables} />
   211→    </AgentProvider>
   212→  );
   213→}
   214→

    34→    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR / "assets"), name="static-assets")

    39→        file_path = STATIC_DIR / full_path

## Section 1: Backend Configuration & Langfuse Client Setup

LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
LANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)
```

**New file: `backend/app/tracing.py`** — Langfuse client wrapper:
- Initialize Langfuse only if `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True
- Verify connection with `auth_check()` at startup
- Expose `get_langfuse_client()` that returns the client or `None`
- Expose `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` that returns the base dashboard URL

```
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com
```

## Section 2: Backend Tracing in Agent Loop

**Modified: `backend/app/agent.py`** — instrument `stream_chat()` with Langfuse:

The tracing wraps around the existing streaming logic without changing the SSE protocol:

```
Trace: "agent-chat" (per POST /api/chat request)
├── metadata: { session_id, model, user_message }
├── Generation: "llm-turn-1" (LLM thinking + text output)
│   └── input: user message, output: thinking + text content
├── Span: "tool-execute_sql" (each tool call)
│   └── input: { sql }, output: { columns, rows, rowCount } or { error }
├── Generation: "llm-turn-2" (if agent continues after tool)
│   └── ...
└── (repeats for multi-turn agent loops)
```

Key design decisions:
- **Tracing is conditional**: If Langfuse is not configured, zero overhead — no spans created, no imports fail
- **Trace per request**: Each `POST /api/chat` call gets one trace with the session_id as the Langfuse session ID
- **Tool spans are children of the trace**: Each `ToolUseBlock` gets a span with the SQL query as input and the result/error as output
- **LLM content captured at generation level**: Thinking text + answer text captured in generation observations
- **No changes to SSE protocol**: The frontend receives the exact same events as before

Does this tracing structure look right?

## Section 3: Backend API Endpoint

**New file: `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py`**:

```
GET /api/langfuse/status
Response: { "enabled": true, "dashboardUrl": "https://cloud.langfuse.com" }
       or { "enabled": false, "dashboardUrl": null }
```

- Called once by the frontend on app load
- Returns whether Langfuse is configured and the dashboard base URL
- No secrets exposed — only the public-facing dashboard URL

**`backend/app/main.py`** — register the new route:

app.include_router(langfuse.router)
```

## Section 4: Frontend — Langfuse Status Fetching

**Modified: `frontend/src/App.tsx`**:

- On app load (alongside the existing health check + table refresh), fetch `GET /api/langfuse/status`
- Store `{ enabled: boolean, dashboardUrl: string | null }` in state
- Pass this state down to `AgentPanel` (or via context)

```typescript
// In App component's useEffect:
const langfuseRes = await fetch('/api/langfuse/status');
const langfuseData = await langfuseRes.json();
setLangfuseStatus(langfuseData); // { enabled, dashboardUrl }
```

This is lightweight — one extra fetch on app load, no polling.

## Section 5: Frontend — "Langfuse Traces" Button in Agent Mode Header

**Modified: `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx`**:

The button sits in the agent panel header, right next to "Agent Mode" title text:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Agent Mode    [Langfuse Traces ↗]   [Clear] │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                             │
│  (messages area)                            │
│                                             │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ [Chat input...]                    [Send]   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

Button behavior:
- **When Langfuse is enabled**: Styled as a subtle link-style button (small, gray text with external link icon), opens `dashboardUrl` in a new tab
- **When Langfuse is not enabled**: Button is hidden (not shown at all, since there's nothing to link to)
- Positioned between the "Agent Mode" title and the "Clear" button

## Design Summary

Here's the complete design:

**Backend:**
1. `config.py` — add `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL`, `LANGFUSE_ENABLED`
2. New `tracing.py` — Langfuse client init, conditional tracing helpers
3. `agent.py` — wrap `stream_chat()` with trace + generation/tool spans (conditional, zero overhead when disabled)
4. New `routes/langfuse.py` — `GET /api/langfuse/status` → `{ enabled, dashboardUrl }`
5. `main.py` — register langfuse router
6. `.env.example` — add optional Langfuse vars
7. `pyproject.toml` — add `langfuse` dependency

**Frontend:**
1. `App.tsx` — fetch Langfuse status on load, pass to AgentPanel
2. `AgentPanel.tsx` — "Langfuse Traces" button next to title, always visible, disabled when not configured, opens dashboard URL in new tab when enabled
3. `AgentPanel.css` — styles for the new button (enabled + disabled states)

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-design.md",
  "content": "# Langfuse Integration Design\n\n## Goal\n\nAdd Langfuse observability tracing to the agent backend and a \"Langfuse Traces\" button to the agent mode UI. Langfuse is optional — when not configured, tracing is skipped with zero overhead and the button is shown disabled.\n\n## Approach\n\n**@observe decorator + manual spans** — wraps the existing `stream_chat()` function with Langfuse tracing. Each agent chat request produces one trace containing LLM generation spans and tool execution spans. No changes to the SSE streaming protocol.\n\n## Backend Changes\n\n### 1. Configuration (`backend/app/config.py`)\n\nAdd environment variables:\n\n```python\nLANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv(\"LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY\", \"\")\nLANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv(\"LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY\", \"\")\nLANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv(\"LANGFUSE_BASE_URL\", \"https://cloud.langfuse.com\")\nLANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)\n```\n\nUpdate `backend/.env.example`:\n\n```\nLANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=\nLANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=\nLANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com\n```\n\n### 2. Tracing Module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)\n\nNew module that:\n\n- Initializes Langfuse client only when `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True\n- Verifies connection with `auth_check()` at startup (logs warning on failure)\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_client()` returning the client or `None`\n- Exposes `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` returning the base URL or `None`\n\n### 3. Agent Instrumentation (`backend/app/agent.py`)\n\nWrap `stream_chat()` with conditional Langfuse tracing:\n\n**Trace hierarchy:**\n\n```\nTrace: \"agent-chat\"\n  metadata: { session_id, model, user_message (truncated) }\n  ├── Generation: \"llm-turn-N\"\n  │   input: messages context\n  │   output: thinking + text content\n  │   usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens }\n  ├── Span: \"tool-execute_sql\"\n  │   input: { sql }\n  │   output: { columns, rows (truncated), rowCount } or { error }\n  └── (repeats for multi-turn agent loops)\n```\n\nKey constraints:\n- Tracing is conditional — if `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None`, no spans are created\n- Zero changes to the SSE event protocol\n- Trace captures the session_id as the Langfuse session ID for grouping multi-turn conversations\n- Tool results are truncated in traces to avoid sending large datasets to Langfuse\n\n### 4. API Endpoint (`backend/app/routes/langfuse.py`)\n\n```\nGET /api/langfuse/status\nResponse: { \"enabled\": true, \"dashboardUrl\": \"https://cloud.langfuse.com\" }\n       or { \"enabled\": false, \"dashboardUrl\": null }\n```\n\nNo secrets exposed. Called once by frontend on app load.\n\n### 5. App Setup (`backend/app/main.py`)\n\nRegister the new langfuse router.\n\n### 6. Dependencies (`backend/pyproject.toml`)\n\nAdd `langfuse` package.\n\n## Frontend Changes\n\n### 1. Langfuse Status Fetching (`frontend/src/App.tsx`)\n\nOn app load (alongside existing health check), fetch `GET /api/langfuse/status`. Store `{ enabled: boolean, dashboardUrl: string | null }` in state and pass to `AgentPanel`.\n\n### 2. Langfuse Traces Button (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx`)\n\nAdd \"Langfuse Traces\" button in the agent panel header, between the \"Agent Mode\" title and \"Clear\" button:\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Agent Mode    [Langfuse Traces ↗]    [Clear] │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n```\n\nButton states:\n- **Enabled** (Langfuse configured): Subtle link-style button, opens `dashboardUrl` in new tab\n- **Disabled** (Langfuse not configured): Grayed out, tooltip \"Langfuse not configured\"\n\n### 3. Styles (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css`)\n\nAdd styles for the Langfuse button in both enabled and disabled states. Match existing design language (small text, gray tones, subtle borders).\n\n## Files Changed\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `backend/app/config.py` | Add Langfuse env vars |\n| `backend/app/tracing.py` | **New** — Langfuse client wrapper |\n| `backend/app/agent.py` | Add conditional tracing spans |\n| `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` | **New** — status endpoint |\n| `backend/app/main.py` | Register langfuse router |\n| `backend/pyproject.toml` | Add langfuse dependency |\n| `backend/.env.example` | Add Langfuse vars |\n| `frontend/src/App.tsx` | Fetch Langfuse status, pass to AgentPanel |\n| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` | Add Langfuse Traces button |\n| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css` | Button styles |\n"
}
```

    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, TableInfo, ToolCallResult } from './types';
    11→
    12→interface AgentContextValue {

    14→  isStreaming: boolean;
    15→  sendMessage: (text: string) => void;
    16→  clearMessages: () => void;

    18→
    19→const AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextValue>({
    20→  messages: [],
    21→  isStreaming: false,
    22→  sendMessage: () => {},
    23→  clearMessages: () => {},
    24→});
    25→
    26→function generateId() {

    28→}
    29→
    30→export function AgentProvider({
    31→  children,
    32→  refreshTables,
    33→}: {
    34→  children: ReactNode;
    35→  tables: TableInfo[];
    36→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    37→}) {
    38→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    39→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    40→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    41→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    42→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    43→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    44→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    45→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    46→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    47→
    48→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    49→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    50→    if (!text) return;
    51→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    52→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    53→    setMessages((prev) =>
    54→      prev.map((m) =>
    55→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    56→      )
    57→    );
    58→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    59→  }, []);
    60→
    61→  const sendMessage = useCallback(

    64→
    65→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {

    67→        role: 'user',

    70→

    72→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;

    76→        content: '',

    79→      };
    80→
    81→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    82→      setIsStreaming(true);
    83→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    84→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    85→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    86→
    87→      const controller = new AbortController();
    88→      abortRef.current = controller;
    89→
    90→      await runAgentLoop(
    91→        text,
    92→        sessionIdRef.current,
    93→        {
    94→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    95→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    96→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    97→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    98→                flushText();
    99→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   100→              }, 50);
   101→            }
   102→          },
   103→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   104→            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.
   105→            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.
   106→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   107→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   108→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   109→            }
   110→            flushText();
   111→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   112→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   113→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   114→            }
   115→            setMessages((prev) =>
   116→              prev.map((m) =>
   117→                m.id === assistantId
   118→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   119→                  : m
   120→              )
   121→            );
   122→          },
   123→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   124→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   125→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   126→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   127→            }
   128→            flushText();
   129→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   130→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   131→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   132→            }
   133→            // Add a pending tool segment so the input is shown immediately
   134→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   135→              type: 'tool',
   136→              toolResult: pending,
   137→            });
   138→            setMessages((prev) =>
   139→              prev.map((m) =>
   140→                m.id === assistantId
   141→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   142→                  : m
   143→              )
   144→            );
   145→          },
   146→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   147→            // Merge result into pending tool segment (keep input info, add output)
   148→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   149→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   150→            );
   151→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   152→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   153→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   154→                type: 'tool',
   155→                toolResult: {
   156→                  ...pending,
   157→                  ...result,
   158→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   159→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   160→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   161→                },
   162→              };
   163→            } else {
   164→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   165→            }
   166→            setMessages((prev) =>
   167→              prev.map((m) =>
   168→                m.id === assistantId
   169→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   170→                  : m
   171→              )
   172→            );
   173→            refreshTables();
   174→          },
   175→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   176→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   177→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   178→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   179→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   180→            }
   181→            flushText();
   182→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   183→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   184→                type: 'answer',
   185→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   186→              });
   187→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   188→            }
   189→            setMessages((prev) =>
   190→              prev.map((m) =>
   191→                m.id === assistantId
   192→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   193→                  : m
   194→              )
   195→            );
   196→            setIsStreaming(false);
   197→            abortRef.current = null;
   198→          },
   199→          onError: (error) => {
   200→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   201→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   202→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   203→            }
   204→            flushText();
   205→            setMessages((prev) =>
   206→              prev.map((m) =>
   207→                m.id === assistantId
   208→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   209→                  : m
   210→              )
   211→            );
   212→            setIsStreaming(false);
   213→            abortRef.current = null;
   214→          },
   215→        },
   216→        controller.signal
   217→      );
   218→    },
   219→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables]
   220→  );
   221→
   222→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   223→    if (abortRef.current) {
   224→      abortRef.current.abort();
   225→    }
   226→    setMessages([]);
   227→    setIsStreaming(false);
   228→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   229→  }, []);
   230→
   231→  return (
   232→    <AgentContext.Provider
   233→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, clearMessages }}
   234→    >
   235→      {children}
   236→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   237→  );
   238→}
   239→
   240→export function useAgent() {
   241→  return useContext(AgentContext);
   242→}
   243→
```

```
     1→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
     9→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    10→}
    11→
    12→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    13→
    14→export async function runAgentLoop(
    15→  message: string,
    16→  sessionId: string | null,
    17→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    18→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    19→): Promise<void> {
    20→  try {
    21→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    22→      method: 'POST',
    23→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    24→      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    25→      signal,
    26→    });
    27→
    28→    if (!response.ok) {
    29→      const errorText = await response.text();
    30→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
    31→      return;
    32→    }
    33→
    34→    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    35→    if (!reader) {
    36→      callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    37→      return;
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    41→    let buffer = '';
    42→
    43→    while (true) {
    44→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    45→      if (done) break;
    46→
    47→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    48→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    49→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    50→
    51→      let eventType = '';
    52→      for (const line of lines) {
    53→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    54→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    55→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    56→          const data = line.slice(6);
    57→          try {
    58→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    59→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);
    60→          } catch {
    61→            // Skip malformed JSON
    62→          }
    63→          eventType = '';
    64→        }
    65→      }
    66→    }
    67→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    68→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    69→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    70→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    71→  }
    72→}
    73→
    74→function handleSSEEvent(
    75→  eventType: string,
    76→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
    77→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    78→) {
    79→  switch (eventType) {
    80→    case 'thinking':
    81→    case 'answer':
    82→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
    83→      break;
    84→    case 'thinking_done':
    85→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
    86→      break;
    87→    case 'tool_call':
    88→      callbacks.onToolCall({
    89→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
    90→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
    91→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    92→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
    93→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
    94→        columns: [],
    95→        rows: [],
    96→        rowCount: 0,
    97→      });
    98→      break;
    99→    case 'tool_result': {
   100→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   101→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   102→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   103→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   104→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   105→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   106→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   107→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   108→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   109→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   110→      };
   111→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   112→      break;
   113→    }
   114→    case 'done':
   115→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   116→      break;
   117→    case 'error':
   118→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   119→      break;
   120→  }
   121→}
   122→
```

```
     1→# Langfuse Integration Design
     2→
     3→## Goal
     4→
     5→Add Langfuse observability tracing to the agent backend and a "Langfuse Traces" button to the agent mode UI. Langfuse is optional — when not configured, tracing is skipped with zero overhead and the button is shown disabled.
     6→
     7→## Approach
     8→
     9→**@observe decorator + manual spans** — wraps the existing `stream_chat()` function with Langfuse tracing. Each agent chat request produces one trace containing LLM generation spans and tool execution spans. No changes to the SSE streaming protocol.
    10→
    11→## Backend Changes
    12→
    13→### 1. Configuration (`backend/app/config.py`)
    14→
    15→Add environment variables:
    16→
    17→```python
    18→LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "")
    19→LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY", "")
    20→LANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
    21→LANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)
    22→```
    23→
    24→Update `backend/.env.example`:
    25→
    26→```
    27→LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=
    28→LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=
    29→LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com
    30→```
    31→
    32→### 2. Tracing Module (`backend/app/tracing.py`)
    33→
    34→New module that:
    35→
    36→- Initializes Langfuse client only when `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` is True
    37→- Verifies connection with `auth_check()` at startup (logs warning on failure)
    38→- Exposes `get_langfuse_client()` returning the client or `None`
    39→- Exposes `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()` returning the base URL or `None`
    40→
    41→### 3. Agent Instrumentation (`backend/app/agent.py`)
    42→
    43→Wrap `stream_chat()` with conditional Langfuse tracing:
    44→
    45→**Trace hierarchy:**
    46→
    47→```
    48→Trace: "agent-chat"
    49→  metadata: { session_id, model, user_message (truncated) }
    50→  ├── Generation: "llm-turn-N"
    51→  │   input: messages context
    52→  │   output: thinking + text content
    53→  │   usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens }
    54→  ├── Span: "tool-execute_sql"
    55→  │   input: { sql }
    56→  │   output: { columns, rows (truncated), rowCount } or { error }
    57→  └── (repeats for multi-turn agent loops)
    58→```
    59→
    60→Key constraints:
    61→- Tracing is conditional — if `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None`, no spans are created
    62→- Zero changes to the SSE event protocol
    63→- Trace captures the session_id as the Langfuse session ID for grouping multi-turn conversations
    64→- Tool results are truncated in traces to avoid sending large datasets to Langfuse
    65→
    66→### 4. API Endpoint (`backend/app/routes/langfuse.py`)
    67→
    68→```
    69→GET /api/langfuse/status
    70→Response: { "enabled": true, "dashboardUrl": "https://cloud.langfuse.com" }
    71→       or { "enabled": false, "dashboardUrl": null }
    72→```
    73→
    74→No secrets exposed. Called once by frontend on app load.
    75→
    76→### 5. App Setup (`backend/app/main.py`)
    77→
    78→Register the new langfuse router.
    79→
    80→### 6. Dependencies (`backend/pyproject.toml`)
    81→
    82→Add `langfuse` package.
    83→
    84→## Frontend Changes
    85→
    86→### 1. Langfuse Status Fetching (`frontend/src/App.tsx`)
    87→
    88→On app load (alongside existing health check), fetch `GET /api/langfuse/status`. Store `{ enabled: boolean, dashboardUrl: string | null }` in state and pass to `AgentPanel`.
    89→
    90→### 2. Langfuse Traces Button (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx`)
    91→
    92→Add "Langfuse Traces" button in the agent panel header, between the "Agent Mode" title and "Clear" button:
    93→
    94→```
    95→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    96→│ Agent Mode    [Langfuse Traces ↗]    [Clear] │
    97→├──────────────────────────────────────────────┤
    98→```
    99→
   100→Button states:
   101→- **Enabled** (Langfuse configured): Subtle link-style button, opens `dashboardUrl` in new tab
   102→- **Disabled** (Langfuse not configured): Grayed out, tooltip "Langfuse not configured"
   103→
   104→### 3. Styles (`frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css`)
   105→
   106→Add styles for the Langfuse button in both enabled and disabled states. Match existing design language (small text, gray tones, subtle borders).
   107→
   108→## Files Changed
   109→
   110→| File | Change |
   111→|---|---|
   112→| `backend/app/config.py` | Add Langfuse env vars |
   113→| `backend/app/tracing.py` | **New** — Langfuse client wrapper |
   114→| `backend/app/agent.py` | Add conditional tracing spans |
   115→| `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` | **New** — status endpoint |
   116→| `backend/app/main.py` | Register langfuse router |
   117→| `backend/pyproject.toml` | Add langfuse dependency |
   118→| `backend/.env.example` | Add Langfuse vars |
   119→| `frontend/src/App.tsx` | Fetch Langfuse status, pass to AgentPanel |
   120→| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` | Add Langfuse Traces button |
   121→| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css` | Button styles |
   122→
```

`backend/app/tracing.py`:\n\n```python\nimport logging\n\nfrom app.config import (\n    LANGFUSE_ENABLED,\n    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY,\n    LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY,\n    LANGFUSE_BASE_URL,\n)\n\nlogger = LANGFUSE_ENABLED:\n        logger.info(\"Langfuse not configured, tracing disabled\")\n        return\n\n    try:\n        from langfuse import Langfuse\n\n        _langfuse_client = Langfuse(\n            REDACTED,\n            secret_key=LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY,\n            base_url=LANGFUSE_BASE_URL,\n        )\n        if _langfuse_client.auth_check():\n            logger.info(\"Langfuse connected \"\"\"Stream agent chat responses as SSE events.\"\"\"\n    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()\n\n    logger.info(\"Using model: %s\", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)\n    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(\n        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),\n        mcp_servers={\"duckdb\": duckdb_server},\n        allowed_tools=[\"mcp__duckdb__execute_sql\"],\n        permission_mode=\"bypassPermissions\",\n        max_turns=20,\n        include_partial_messages=True,\n    )\n\n    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)\n    actual_session_id = session_id\n\n    # Langfuse tracing (conditional)\n    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()\n    trace = None\n    current_generation = None\n    llm_turn = 0\n    accumulated_thinking = \"\"\n    accumulated_answer = \"\"\n\n    if langfuse:\n        trace = langfuse.trace(\n            name=\"agent-chat\",\n            session_id=session_id or \"default\",\n            input={\"message\": message[:500]},\n            metadata={\"model\": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},\n        )\n\n    try:\n        await client.connect()\n        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or \"default\")\n\n        current_text = \"\"\n        has_tool_calls = False\n        thinking_sent = False\n        has_thinking = False\n        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()\n        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}\n\n        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():\n            try:\n                msg = parse_message(raw_data)\n            except MessageParseError as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Skipping unrecognized message: %s\", e)\n                continue\n\n            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):\n                event = msg.event\n                if not actual_session_id:\n                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id\n\n                event_type = event.get(\"type\", \"\")\n\n                if event_type == \"content_block_delta\":\n                    delta = event.get(\"delta\", {})\n                    delta_type = delta.get(\"type\", \"\")\n                    if delta_type == \"thinking_delta\":\n                        text = delta.get(\"thinking\", \"\")\n                        if text:\n                            current_text += text\n                            accumulated_thinking += text\n                            yield f\"event: thinking\\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\\n\\n\"\n                    elif delta_type == \"text_delta\":\n                        text = delta.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                        current_text += text\n                        accumulated_answer += text\n                        event_name = \"thinking\" if not has_tool_calls else \"answer\"\n                        yield for this LLM turn\n                        if trace:\n                            llm_turn += 1\n                            current_generation = trace.generation(\n                                name=f\"llm-turn-{llm_turn}\",\n                                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n                                input={\"message\": message[:500]} if llm_turn == 1 else {\"continued\": True},\n                            )\n                    elif block_type == \"text\":\n                        if has_thinking:\n                            yield f\"event: thinking_done\\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\\n\\n\"\n                        elif trace and not current_generation:\n                            # Text block without thinking — start generation\n                            llm_turn += 1\n                            current_generation = trace.generation(\n                                name=f\"llm-turn-{llm_turn}\",\n                                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n                                input={\"message\": message[:500]} if llm_turn == 1 else {\"continued\": True},\n                            )\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_use\":\n                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:\n                            thinking_sent = True\n                        has_tool_calls = True\n                        # End current generation before tool use\n                        if current_generation:\n                            current_generation.end(\n                                output={\"thinking\": accumulated_thinking[:1000], \"text\": accumulated_answer[:1000]},\n                            )\n                            current_generation = None\n                            accumulated_thinking = \"\"\n                            accumulated_answer = \"\"\n\n            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):\n                if not actual_session_id:\n                    actual_session_id = \"default\"\n\n                for block in msg.content:\n                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):\n                        has_tool_calls = True\n tool_name}\n                        if sql:\n                            tool_call_data[\"sql\"] = sql\n                        if command:\n                            tool_call_data[\"command\"] = command\n                        if not sql and not command:\n                            tool_call_data[\"input\"] = block.input\n                        yield f\"event: tool_call\\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\\n\\n\"\n\n                        # For SQL tools, execute query for structured results\n                        if sql:\n                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)\n\n                            # Create tool span in trace\n                            tool_span = trace.span(\n yield f\"event: tool_result\\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\\n\\n\"\n                                if tool_span:\n ## Report

### What I Implemented

Exactly what the task specified -- added Langfuse dependency and configuration to the backend:

1. **Added `langfuse = "^3.0.0"` to `backend/pyproject.toml`** (line 17) in the `[tool.poetry.dependencies]` section.

2. **Added Langfuse config variables to `backend/app/config.py`** (lines 9-12):

3. **Added Langfuse env vars to `backend/.env.example`** (lines 3-5):

   - `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=`
   - `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com`

4. **Ran `poetry lock --no-update` and `poetry install`** -- langfuse 3.14.4 installed successfully along with 22 transitive dependencies.

### What I Tested and Results

- **`poetry install`**: Succeeded. Langfuse 3.14.4 and all its dependencies installed cleanly.

### Self-Review Findings

- All changes match the spec exactly -- no overbuilding, no deviations.

---

# CODE REVIEW: Task 1 - Add Langfuse Dependency and Configuration

**Commits Reviewed:** 04f2f12 → 30aaf96  
**Date:** February 20, 2026  
**Review Focus:** Code quality, pattern adherence, and alignment with implementation plan

---

## STRENGTHS

1. **Correct Implementation Against Plan**: The implementation precisely matches the Langfuse integration plan specifications. All three required files were modified with the exact configurations specified.

2. **Smart Configuration Design**: The `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` flag is a well-designed feature that enables optional tracing without overhead. It correctly checks for both public and secret keys before enabling, ensuring zero runtime cost when unconfigured.

3. **Consistent Naming Convention**: Configuration variable names follow the existing project convention:
   - Matches the `ANTHROPIC_*` pattern already established
   - Uses uppercase constants for module-level variables
   - Clear, descriptive names

4. **Proper Dependency Versioning**: 
   - Uses `langfuse = "^3.0.0"` which is the planned version
   - Allows minor and patch updates within the major version constraint
   - Poetry lock file properly generated with all transitive dependencies

5. **Sensible Defaults**:
   - `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY` and `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY` default to empty strings (safe)
   - `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL` defaults to the official Langfuse cloud URL
   - Defaults allow graceful degradation when unconfigured

6. **Clean Code Structure**:
   - Blank line spacing between ANTHROPIC and LANGFUSE configs improves readability
   - Minimal changes - only necessary additions
   - No unnecessary modifications or refactoring

7. **Example File Best Practice**: `.env.example` file properly documents all available configuration options with sensible placeholder values.

8. **Functional Verification**: Configuration imports correctly and all variables properly resolve with expected types.

---

## ISSUES & RECOMMENDATIONS

### CRITICAL
None identified.

### IMPORTANT
None identified.

### SUGGESTIONS (Nice to Have)

**Suggestion 1: Add Configuration Documentation Comment**  
**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/config.py`  
**Severity:** Minor - Documentation Enhancement

The Langfuse configuration section would benefit from an inline comment explaining the optional nature:

Current:
```python
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
LANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)
```

Suggested enhancement:
```python
# Langfuse configuration (optional - zero overhead when disabled)
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY", "")
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL = os.getenv("LANGFUSE_BASE_URL", "https://cloud.langfuse.com")
LANGFUSE_ENABLED = bool(LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY and LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY)
```

**Why:** Helps future developers understand that Langfuse is optional and won't impact performance when disabled. Consistent with good documentation practices.

---

## PLAN ALIGNMENT ANALYSIS

## Report

### What I Implemented

Created `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py` -- a Langfuse tracing module with the following components:

- **`_init_langfuse()`** -- Internal function that conditionally initializes the Langfuse client. It checks `LANGFUSE_ENABLED` from config, and if enabled, imports Langfuse, creates the client with the configured keys/URL, and runs `auth_check()` to verify connectivity. Gracefully handles failures by setting the client to `None` and logging warnings.
- **`get_langfuse_client()`** -- Public accessor that returns the Langfuse client instance or `None` if not configured.
- **`get_langfuse_dashboard_url()`** -- Returns the Langfuse base URL when the client is active, or `None` otherwise.
- Module-level `_init_langfuse()` call triggers initialization on first import.

None. The module is ready for downstream consumers (Task 3 and Task 4).
