---

## Report

### What was implemented

Added conditional Langfuse tracing instrumentation to `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py`. The changes add observability to the agent chat stream without modifying the SSE event protocol.

**Specific additions:**

1. **Import**: Added `from app.tracing import get_langfuse_client` (line 20)

2. **Helper function**: Added `_truncate(text, max_len=1000)` (lines 48-52) to safely truncate text for trace data

3. **Trace creation** (lines 88-101): At the start of `stream_chat()`, gets the Langfuse client and conditionally creates a trace named `"agent-chat"` with the user message (truncated to 500 chars), model metadata, and session ID

4. **Generation spans** (lines 109-146): Two inner functions `_start_generation()` and `_end_generation()` manage LLM turn tracking:
   - `_start_generation()` creates a `trace.generation()` span named `"llm-turn-N"` with model info and input data
   - `_end_generation()` ends the span with accumulated thinking/answer text (truncated to 1000 chars)
   - Generations are started on `content_block_start` with type `"thinking"` or `"text"` (lines 194-202)
   - Generations are ended before `tool_use` blocks (lines 204-206)

5. **Tool spans** (lines 237-271): SQL tool executions are wrapped in `trace.span()` named `"tool-{tool_name}"` with SQL input and result metadata (rowCount, columns) or error output -- notably, full row data is NOT included in the trace

6. **Cleanup in finally block** (lines 302-319): Ends any open generation span, updates the trace output with the final session ID, and flushes Langfuse

### Trace hierarchy

```
Trace: "agent-chat"
  metadata: { model }, input: { message (truncated) }
  session_id: from the chat session
  +-- Generation: "llm-turn-1"
  |     model, input: { message }, output: { thinking, text }
  +-- Span: "tool-{name}"
  |     input: { sql }, output: { rowCount, columns } or { error }
  +-- Generation: "llm-turn-2" (if agent continues)
  +-- ...
```

### What was tested and results

- **Syntax validation**: `ast.parse()` confirms no syntax errors
- **Import structure verification**: All imports parsed correctly, including the new `app.tracing` import
- **Commit succeeded**: Clean working tree after commit

### Files changed

- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` -- 128 lines added, 0 removed

### Self-review findings

All design constraints are satisfied:

1. **All tracing is conditional**: Every Langfuse API call is gated behind `if langfuse:`, `if trace:`, `if current_generation:`, or `if tool_span:` checks. The `_start_generation()` function has an early return if `trace` is None. The accumulated text string appends (`accumulated_thinking += text`, `accumulated_answer += text`) happen unconditionally but are harmless -- they are just string concatenation with negligible overhead.

2. **Zero changes to SSE protocol**: All 10 `yield` statements are byte-identical to the original. No new events, no changed event names, no modified payloads.

3. **Text truncation**: Message input capped at 500 chars, thinking/answer output capped at 1000 chars, tool spans exclude full row data (only rowCount and column names).

4. **Error resilience**: Every Langfuse API call is wrapped in `try/except Exception` with `logger.debug()` so tracing failures never affect the chat stream.

### Issues or concerns

None. The implementation is straightforward and defensive. If Langfuse is not configured (no keys set), `get_langfuse_client()` returns `None` and all tracing is skipped with zero overhead beyond the initial function call and a few `if None:` checks.

    14→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError

    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    25→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    26→    tables = db.list_tables()
    27→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    28→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    29→
    30→Guidelines:
    31→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    32→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    33→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    34→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    35→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    36→"""
    37→    if not tables:
    38→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    39→    else:
    40→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    41→        for table in tables:
    42→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    43→            for col in table["columns"]:
    44→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    45→    return prompt
    46→
    47→
    48→def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int = 1000) -> str:
    49→    """Truncate text for trace data, appending '...' if trimmed."""
    50→    if len(text) <= max_len:
    51→        return text
    52→    return text[:max_len] + "..."
    53→
    54→
    55→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
    56→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
    57→    if content is None:
    58→        return ""
    59→    if isinstance(content, str):
    60→        return content
    61→    if isinstance(content, list):
    62→        parts = []
    63→        for item in content:
    64→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
    65→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
    66→        return "\n".join(parts)
    67→    return str(content)
    68→
    69→
    70→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    71→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    72→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    73→
    74→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
    75→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    76→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
    77→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    78→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    79→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    80→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    81→        max_turns=20,
    82→        include_partial_messages=True,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    86→    actual_session_id = session_id

    88→    # --- Langfuse tracing setup (conditional) ---

    90→    trace = None

    93→            trace = langfuse.trace(
    94→                name="agent-chat",
    95→                input={"message": _truncate(message, 500)},
    96→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
    97→                session_id=session_id or "default",
    98→            )
    99→        except Exception as e:
   100→            logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   101→            trace = None

   105→    turn_number = 0

   107→    accumulated_answer = ""
   108→
   109→    def _end_generation():
   110→        """End the current generation span, recording accumulated text."""
   111→        nonlocal current_generation, accumulated_thinking, accumulated_answer
   112→        if current_generation:
   113→            try:

## Report

### What I Implemented

**Task 5 -- Langfuse status fetching in App.tsx** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/App.tsx`):
- Added a `LangfuseStatus` interface with `enabled: boolean` and `dashboardUrl: string | null`.
- Added `langfuseStatus` state in the `App` component, initialized to `{ enabled: false, dashboardUrl: null }`.
- Added a non-critical fetch to `/api/langfuse/status` inside the existing `useEffect`, right after `await refreshTables()`. If the fetch fails, it is silently caught so it does not block the app from loading.
- Extended `AppContent`'s props type to include `langfuseStatus: LangfuseStatus`.
- Passed `langfuseStatus` from `App` -> `AppContent` -> `AgentPanel`.

**Task 6 -- Langfuse Traces button in AgentPanel** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` and `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css`):
- Added `LangfuseStatus` and `AgentPanelProps` interfaces.
- Changed `AgentPanel` from a no-props function to accept `{ langfuseStatus }`.
- Added a "Langfuse Traces" button in the header, wrapped in an `.agent-panel__actions` div alongside the existing Clear button.
  - The button is disabled and visually dimmed when `langfuseStatus.enabled` is false.
  - When enabled and clicked, it opens `langfuseStatus.dashboardUrl` in a new tab with `noopener,noreferrer`.
  - The button has a tooltip showing either "Open Langfuse dashboard" or "Langfuse not configured".
- Added CSS styles: `.agent-panel__actions` (flex container with gap), `.agent-panel__langfuse` (base button style), `.agent-panel__langfuse:hover:not(:disabled)` (hover state), and `.agent-panel__langfuse--disabled` (reduced opacity, not-allowed cursor).

### What I Tested and Results

- **TypeScript compilation**: Ran `npx tsc --noEmit` -- passed with zero errors.
- **Git status**: Working tree is clean after both commits.

### Files Changed

1. `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/App.tsx` -- +17 lines, -3 lines
2. `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` -- +28 lines, -2 lines (approx)
3. `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css` -- +26 lines

### Commits

1. `7f37eed` -- `feat: fetch langfuse status on app load and pass to AgentPanel`
2. `af7ee29` -- `feat: add Langfuse Traces button to agent panel header`

### Self-Review Findings

- The `LangfuseStatus` interface is defined in both `App.tsx` and `AgentPanel.tsx`. This is intentional per the task spec rather than sharing a single type from a `types.ts` file. This duplication is acceptable for a small interface; if it grows or is needed elsewhere, it could be extracted into a shared types file.
- The Langfuse status fetch uses a nested try/catch inside the main useEffect's try block, which correctly prevents a Langfuse failure from affecting the health check or table loading flow.
- The button correctly handles the case where `enabled` is true but `dashboardUrl` could theoretically be null (the `onClick` handler checks `dashboardUrl` before calling `window.open`).

### Issues or Concerns

None. All changes compile cleanly and follow the specification exactly.

# Langfuse Integration -- Final Code Review

## 1. Plan Alignment Analysis

### Design Document: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/docs/plans/2026-02-20-langfuse-integration-design.md`

**Files Changed (Plan vs. Actual):**

| Planned File | Actual | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `backend/app/config.py` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/app/tracing.py` (new) | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/app/agent.py` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` (new) | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/app/main.py` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/pyproject.toml` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `backend/.env.example` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `frontend/src/App.tsx` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` | Implemented | MATCH |
| `frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css` | Implemented | MATCH |

All 10 planned files were touched. No unexpected files were changed beyond `poetry.lock` (expected transitive dependency).

**Approach Deviation:** The design document says **"@observe decorator + manual spans"** in the Approach section, but the implementation uses **manual spans only** (no `@observe` decorator). This is a justified departure -- the `@observe` decorator does not integrate well with async generators (the `stream_chat()` function yields SSE events), so manual span management is the correct choice. The design doc itself describes manual tracing in the detailed Agent Instrumentation section, so the "Approach" heading is slightly misleading, but the actual spec was followed faithfully.

**Trace Hierarchy -- Plan vs. Actual:**

The plan calls for:
```
Trace: "agent-chat"
  metadata: { session_id, model, user_message (truncated) }
  +-- Generation: "llm-turn-N"
  +-- Span: "tool-execute_sql"
```

The implementation at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` delivers exactly this:
- Lines 93-101: Trace creation with `name="agent-chat"`, truncated input, model metadata, session_id
- Lines 139-143: Generation span `"llm-turn-{turn_number}"` with model info
- Lines 241-244: Tool span `"tool-{tool_name}"` with SQL input
- Lines 256-259: Tool span ended with `rowCount` and `columns` on success
- Lines 269-270: Tool span ended with error output on failure

**SSE Protocol:** Verified -- zero changes to SSE event types or data shapes. All tracing code is additive and does not alter any `yield` statements.

**Overall Plan Alignment: EXCELLENT.** Every requirement from the design doc has been implemented.

---

## 2. Code Quality Assessment

### What Was Done Well

**Defensive tracing throughout `agent.py`.** Every Langfuse API call is wrapped in `try/except` with a `logger.debug()` fallback. This means a Langfuse outage or API error can never crash the agent chat stream. Examples at lines 99-101, 120-121, 144-145, 245-246, 260-261, 269-271, 309-313, 316-318. This is thorough and well-executed.

**Clean conditional initialization in `tracing.py`.** The lazy import of `from langfuse import Langfuse` at line 24 inside `_init_langfuse()` means the langfuse package is never imported when not configured, achieving the "zero overhead" goal. The `auth_check()` call at line 30 catches bad credentials at startup rather than at first request time.

**Frontend resilience.** The Langfuse status fetch in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/App.tsx` at lines 196-203 is inside a nested try/catch with the comment "Langfuse status fetch is non-critical." The default state at line 177 is `{ enabled: false, dashboardUrl: null }`, so if the fetch fails, the button simply stays disabled. This is correct fail-safe behavior.

**Truncation utility.** The `_truncate()` helper at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` lines 48-52 is simple and consistently applied to all trace data -- message input (500 chars), thinking/answer output (1000 chars default). This prevents accidentally sending large payloads to Langfuse.

**Security.** The `/api/langfuse/status` endpoint at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` returns only `enabled` (boolean) and `dashboardUrl` (the base URL). No API keys, no secret key, no public key are exposed. This matches the design requirement exactly.

### Issues Found

#### IMPORTANT (Should Fix)

**I1. Duplicate `LangfuseStatus` interface definition.**

The `LangfuseStatus` TypeScript interface is defined twice:

- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/App.tsx` lines 13-16:
  ```typescript
  interface LangfuseStatus {
    enabled: boolean;
    dashboardUrl: string | null;
  }
  ```

- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` lines 7-10:
  ```typescript
  interface LangfuseStatus {
    enabled: boolean;
    dashboardUrl: string | null;
  }
  ```

This should be defined once in a shared `types.ts` file (the project already has `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts`) and imported in both locations. Duplication creates a maintenance burden -- if the API response shape changes, two files must be updated.

**Recommendation:** Move the interface to `types.ts` and import it in both files.

---

**I2. `langfuse` is a hard dependency even when not used.**

In `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/pyproject.toml` line 17:
```toml
langfuse = "^3.0.0"
```

The langfuse package (and its transitive dependencies -- 653 lines of poetry.lock changes) is always installed, even for deployments that will never use tracing. The design says "zero overhead when not configured," and the runtime behavior achieves this via lazy import, but the install-time overhead is significant.

**Recommendation:** Consider making it an optional dependency group:
```toml
[tool.poetry.extras]
tracing = ["langfuse"]
```
This is a suggestion, not a blocker, since the lazy import in `tracing.py` already avoids runtime overhead. However, it would be cleaner for production deployments that do not need Langfuse.

---

**I3. Non-SQL tool executions are not traced with Langfuse spans.**

In `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py`, tool spans are only created for tools with a `sql` parameter (lines 234-271). The `UserMessage` branch at lines 273-290 handles non-SQL tool results for SSE emission but does not create or end any Langfuse span.

The design doc says:
```
Span: "tool-execute_sql"
  input: { sql }
  output: { columns, rows (truncated), rowCount } or { error }
```

This is scoped to SQL tools specifically, so the implementation matches the plan. However, if other MCP tools are added in the future, their executions will be invisible in Langfuse traces. This is worth a comment in the code or a follow-up item.

**Recommendation:** Add a brief comment noting that only SQL tool executions produce Langfuse spans, and that other tools should be instrumented if added later.

---

#### SUGGESTIONS (Nice to Have)

**S1. Generation spans do not capture token usage.**

The design document specifies under the trace hierarchy:
```
Generation: "llm-turn-N"
  usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens }
```

The implementation at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` creates generation spans (line 139) and ends them (line 119) but does not record `usage` data. This is likely because the Claude Agent SDK's streaming events may not expose per-turn token counts in a straightforward way. The omission is understandable but worth noting as a known gap against the design spec.

**Recommendation:** Add a comment or TODO noting that token usage is not yet captured, and investigate whether the SDK exposes this in `ResultMessage` or elsewhere.

---

**S2. `_init_langfuse()` runs at module import time.**

In `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py` line 53:
```python
# Initialize on module import
_init_langfuse()
```

This is fine for the current application structure (FastAPI server), but it means importing the module as a side effect triggers network I/O (`auth_check()` at line 30). This could be surprising in test contexts or CLI tools that import the module. The explicit comment on line 52 documents the intent, which mitigates the concern.

**Recommendation:** No immediate change needed, but if tests are added later, consider a lazy initialization pattern or an explicit `init()` call from `main.py` startup.

---

**S3. The `window.open` call in `AgentPanel.tsx` could be simplified.**

In `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.tsx` lines 33-36:
```typescript
onClick={() => {
  if (langfuseStatus.dashboardUrl) {
    window.open(langfuseStatus.dashboardUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  }
}}
```

The `if` check is technically redundant because the button is already `disabled` when `langfuseStatus.enabled` is false, and `dashboardUrl` is null only when not enabled. However, the defensive check is not harmful and provides a safety net against edge cases where `enabled` is true but `dashboardUrl` is somehow null. This is fine as-is.

---

**S4. No tests for any of the new code.**

The project does not appear to have a test suite (no `tests/` directory outside of `.venv`). While this is a pre-existing gap and not introduced by this feature, the Langfuse integration would particularly benefit from:
- A unit test for `_truncate()` edge cases
- A unit test for `get_langfuse_client()` returning `None` when not configured
- A unit test for the `/api/langfuse/status` endpoint returning the correct shape

**Recommendation:** Consider adding tests as a follow-up if the project begins establishing a test suite.

---

## 3. Architecture and Design Review

**Separation of Concerns:** Good. Tracing initialization is isolated in `tracing.py`, the status endpoint is in its own route file, and agent instrumentation is additive (no existing logic was restructured). The tracing module exposes two clean functions (`get_langfuse_client()`, `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()`) and hides all initialization details.

**Coupling:** The agent module (`agent.py`) depends on `tracing.py` through a single import (`get_langfuse_client`). The coupling is minimal -- a `None` return causes all tracing to be skipped. The frontend coupling is also clean: a single prop (`langfuseStatus`) passed from `App` to `AgentPanel`.

**Scalability:** The manual span approach, while verbose, gives full control over what is traced. If the agent grows more complex (parallel tool calls, multi-model routing), spans can be added without restructuring.

**Module-level global state:** The `_langfuse_client` global in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tracing.py` line 12 is a singleton pattern. This is acceptable for a FastAPI application where the module is loaded once per process. It would not be suitable for a multi-tenant setup, but that is not in scope.

---

## 4. Security Review

- **No secrets exposed:** The `/api/langfuse/status` endpoint returns only boolean + URL. Verified.
- **Environment variables:** Keys are loaded from `.env` via `python-dotenv`, standard practice.
- **Frontend:** `noopener,noreferrer` is used on the external link. Verified at line 35 of `AgentPanel.tsx`.
- **Trace data truncation:** User messages truncated to 500 chars, outputs to 1000 chars. Prevents accidental leakage of large datasets to Langfuse.
- **No CORS changes:** The existing wildcard CORS policy in `main.py` was not modified. (The wildcard policy itself is a pre-existing concern, not introduced by this feature.)

---

## 5. Summary

| Category | Rating |
|---|---|
| Plan Alignment | Excellent -- all 10 files, all requirements met |
| Code Quality | Very Good -- defensive error handling throughout |
| Security | Good -- no secrets exposed, truncation applied |
| Architecture | Good -- clean separation, minimal coupling |
| Error Handling | Excellent -- every Langfuse call wrapped in try/except |
| SSE Protocol Integrity | Verified -- zero changes to streaming behavior |

**Issues to address:**

| ID | Severity | Description |
|---|---|---|
| I1 | Important | Duplicate `LangfuseStatus` interface in `App.tsx` and `AgentPanel.tsx` -- extract to shared `types.ts` |
| I2 | Important | `langfuse` is a hard dependency; consider making it optional |
| I3 | Important | Non-SQL tool executions are not traced; add a comment noting this limitation |
| S1 | Suggestion | Generation spans do not capture token usage as specified in the design doc |
| S2 | Suggestion | Module-level init triggers network I/O on import; document or defer |
| S3 | Suggestion | Defensive null check on `dashboardUrl` in onClick is fine, no change needed |
| S4 | Suggestion | No tests for the new code; consider adding as follow-up |

**Overall Assessment:** This is a well-executed feature implementation that closely follows the design document. The code is defensive, the tracing is truly optional with zero impact on the critical path when disabled, and security requirements are met. The main areas for improvement are the duplicated TypeScript interface (I1) and the hard dependency installation (I2), both of which are straightforward to address. The feature is ready for merge with the I1 fix recommended before or shortly after.

> AGENT

Implementation complete. Here's a summary of everything that was built:

## Langfuse Integration — Complete

**7 commits**, **10 files changed** (4 new, 6 modified)

### Backend
- **`config.py`** — Added `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY`, `LANGFUSE_BASE_URL`, `LANGFUSE_ENABLED`
- **`tracing.py`** (new) — Conditional Langfuse client init with `auth_check()`, exposes `get_langfuse_client()` and `get_langfuse_dashboard_url()`
- **`routes/langfuse.py`** (new) — `GET /api/langfuse/status` returning `{enabled, dashboardUrl}`
- **`agent.py`** — Full agent tracing: trace per request, generation spans per LLM turn, tool spans per SQL execution. All conditional — zero overhead when disabled.
- **`pyproject.toml`** — Added `langfuse ^3.0.0`

### Frontend
- **`types.ts`** — Added shared `LangfuseStatus` interface
- **`App.tsx`** — Fetches `/api/langfuse/status` on load, passes status to AgentPanel
- **`AgentPanel.tsx`** — "Langfuse Traces" button next to "Agent Mode" title, disabled when not configured, opens dashboard in new tab when enabled
- **`AgentPanel.css`** — Button styles (enabled + disabled states)

### To enable Langfuse
Add to `backend/.env`:
```
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=pk-lf-...
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=sk-lf-...
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=https://cloud.langfuse.com
```

> AGENT

Found it. `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` — when uvicorn runs with `--reload`, the import system may be resolving `langfuse` to this local `app/routes/langfuse` module instead of the installed `langfuse` package. But the direct conflict is actually in the `from langfuse import Langfuse` line in `tracing.py` — Python's import resolution with relative paths in certain contexts can pick up local modules.

Let me verify this is the cause:

> AGENT

That's the root cause. Our file `app/routes/langfuse.py` shadows the `langfuse` package. When uvicorn's import system has `app/routes` on the path, `from langfuse import Langfuse` resolves to our route file instead of the installed package.

The fix is simple — rename the route file to avoid the name collision.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01M5JZfd8oxzcgerjr2A8V75

  "description": "Rename langfuse.py to langfuse_status.py to avoid shadowing"
}
```

> AGENT

There's the problem! Look at `sys.path`:

```
/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages  ← NOT where langfuse is installed
```

vs where langfuse actually is:

```
/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/canner/repos/ai-experiment/agent-examples/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages
```

There are **two different venvs**! The `sys.path` includes the venv at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/canner/repos/...` (likely a symlink or an old venv path), but `poetry run` is running from `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/`. The `import langfuse` fails because langfuse is installed in one venv but uvicorn is looking in the other.

> AGENT

Found the root cause! There are **two separate venvs**:

1. `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv` — where langfuse IS installed
2. `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/canner/repos/ai-experiment/agent-examples/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv` — where langfuse is NOT installed

Uvicorn's `sys.path` is using the **second** venv's `site-packages`. This is the real venv (the project directory is likely a symlink or was copied). Let me check:

> AGENT

**There it is!** The uvicorn shebang points to the OLD venv path:

```
#!/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/canner/repos/ai-experiment/agent-examples/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/bin/python
```

The `.venv` was originally created at that path (under `canner/repos/...`) and then the project was moved/copied to `Desktop/duckdb-data-agent`. The venv's script shebangs still point to the old location, which is a separate Python interpreter with its own `site-packages` — where langfuse is NOT installed.

The fix is to recreate the venv:

> TOOL

> AGENT

Fixed. The root cause was **two issues compounding**:

1. **Stale venv**: The `.venv` was created at a different filesystem path (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/canner/repos/...`) and the project was moved to `Desktop/duckdb-data-agent`. The uvicorn binary's shebang still pointed to the old Python, which used the old `site-packages` where langfuse wasn't installed. **Fix**: Recreated the venv with `poetry install`.

2. **Module name shadowing**: `backend/app/routes/langfuse.py` could shadow the installed `langfuse` package if Python's import system resolved it first. **Fix**: Renamed to `langfuse_status.py`.

You'll need to restart `make dev` to pick up the new venv.

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. In v3:
- `langfuse.start_span(name=...)` creates a root span (which is the trace)
- `root_span.update_trace(session_id=..., input=..., metadata=...)` sets trace-level metadata
- `root_span.start_generation(name=..., model=...)` creates child generation
- `root_span.start_span(name=...)` creates child span
- `span.update(output=...)` then `span.end()` to finalize

    25→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    26→    tables = db.list_tables()
    27→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    28→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    29→
    30→Guidelines:
    31→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    32→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    33→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    34→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    35→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    36→"""
    37→    if not tables:
    38→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    39→    else:
    40→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    41→        for table in tables:
    42→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    43→            for col in table["columns"]:
    44→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    45→    return prompt
    46→
    47→
    48→def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int = 1000) -> str:
    49→    """Truncate text for trace data, appending '...' if trimmed."""
    50→    if len(text) <= max_len:
    51→        return text
    52→    return text[:max_len] + "..."
    53→
    54→
    55→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
    56→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
    57→    if content is None:
    58→        return ""
    59→    if isinstance(content, str):
    60→        return content
    61→    if isinstance(content, list):
    62→        parts = []
    63→        for item in content:
    64→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
    65→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
    66→        return "\n".join(parts)
    67→    return str(content)
    68→
    69→
    70→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    71→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""

    73→
    74→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
    75→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(

    77→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    78→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    79→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    80→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    81→        max_turns=20,
    82→        include_partial_messages=True,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    86→    actual_session_id = session_id
    87→
    88→    # --- Langfuse tracing setup (conditional) ---
    89→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
    90→    trace = None
    91→    if langfuse:
    92→        try:
    93→            trace = langfuse.trace(
    94→                name="agent-chat",
    95→                input={"message": _truncate(message, 500)},
    96→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
    97→                session_id=session_id or "default",
    98→            )
    99→        except Exception as e:
   100→            logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   101→            trace = None
   102→
   103→    # Tracing state
   104→    current_generation = None
   105→    turn_number = 0
   106→    accumulated_thinking = ""
   107→    accumulated_answer = ""
   108→
   109→    def _end_generation():
   110→        """End the current generation span, recording accumulated text."""
   111→        nonlocal current_generation, accumulated_thinking, accumulated_answer
   112→        if current_generation:
   113→            try:
   114→                output_data = {}
   115→                if accumulated_thinking:
   116→                    output_data["thinking"] = _truncate(accumulated_thinking)
   117→                if accumulated_answer:
   118→                    output_data["text"] = _truncate(accumulated_answer)
   119→                current_generation.end(output=output_data)
   120→            except Exception as e:
   121→                logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse generation: %s", e)
   122→            current_generation = None
   123→        accumulated_thinking = ""
   124→        accumulated_answer = ""
   125→
   126→    def _start_generation():
   127→        """Start a new generation span for an LLM turn."""
   128→        nonlocal current_generation, turn_number
   129→        if not trace:
   130→            return
   131→        _end_generation()
   132→        turn_number += 1
   133→        try:
   134→            input_data = (
   135→                {"message": _truncate(message, 500)}
   136→                if turn_number == 1
   137→                else {"continued": True}
   138→            )
   139→            current_generation = trace.generation(
   140→                name=f"llm-turn-{turn_number}",
   141→                model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   142→                input=input_data,
   143→            )
   144→        except Exception as e:
   145→            logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse generation: %s", e)
   146→            current_generation = None
   147→
   148→    try:
   149→        await client.connect()
   150→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   151→
   152→        current_text = ""
   153→        has_tool_calls = False
   154→        thinking_sent = False
   155→        has_thinking = False
   156→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   157→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   158→
   159→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
   160→            try:

   162→            except MessageParseError as e:

   164→                continue
   165→
   166→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   167→                event = msg.event
   168→                if not actual_session_id:
   169→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id

   171→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   172→
   173→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   174→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   175→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   176→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":

   179→                            current_text += text

   181→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   182→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":

   185→                        accumulated_answer += text

   188→
   189→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":

   191→                    block_type = block.get("type")

   242→                                        name=f"tool-{tool_name}",
   243→                                        input={"sql": sql},
   244→                                    )
   245→                                except Exception as e:
   246→                                    logger.debug("Failed to create Langfuse tool span: %s", e)
   247→
   248→                            try:
   249→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   250→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   251→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   252→
   253→                                # End tool span with success
   254→                                if tool_span:
   255→                                    try:
   256→                                        tool_span.end(output={
   257→                                            "rowCount": result["rowCount"],
   258→                                            "columns": result["columns"],
   259→                                        })
   260→                                    except Exception as e:
   261→                                        logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse tool span: %s", e)
   262→
   263→                            except Exception as e:
   264→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   265→
   266→                                # End tool span with error
   267→                                if tool_span:
   268→                                    try:
   269→                                        tool_span.end(output={"error": str(e)})
   270→                                    except Exception as ex:
   271→                                        logger.debug("Failed to end Langfuse tool span: %s", ex)
   272→
   273→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   274→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   275→                # Note: only SQL tool executions produce Langfuse spans (see above)
   276→                content = msg.content
   277→                if isinstance(content, list):
   278→                    for block in content:
   279→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   280→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   281→                                continue
   282→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   283→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   284→                            result_data: dict = {
   285→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   286→                                "name": name,
   287→                                "output": output,
   288→                            }
   289→                            if block.is_error:
   290→                                result_data["error"] = output
   291→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   292→
   293→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   294→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   295→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   296→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   297→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   298→                break
   299→
   300→    except Exception as e:
   301→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   302→    finally:
   303→        # End any open generation span
   304→        if trace:
   305→            _end_generation()
   306→
   307→        # Update trace output and flush
   308→        if trace:
   309→            try:
   310→                trace.update(
   311→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   312→                )
   313→            except Exception as e:
   314→                logger.debug("Failed to update Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   315→
   316→        if langfuse:
   317→            try:
   318→                langfuse.flush()
   319→            except Exception as e:
   320→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   321→
   322→        try:
   323→            await client.disconnect()
   324→        except Exception:
   325→            pass
   326→
```

Now I'll fix all the v2 API calls to use v3. The changes are:
- `langfuse.trace(...)` → `langfuse.start_span(name=...)` + `root.update_trace(session_id=..., input=..., metadata=...)`
- `trace.generation(...)` → `trace.start_generation(name=..., model=...)`
- `trace.span(...)` → `trace.start_span(name=..., input=...)`
- `generation.end(output=...)` → `generation.update(output=...)` then `generation.end()`
- `tool_span.end(output=...)` → `tool_span.update(output=...)` then `tool_span.end()`

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was that the code used Langfuse SDK v2 API methods (`.trace()`, `.generation()`, `.span()`, `.end(output=...)`), but the installed `langfuse ^3.0.0` uses a different API:

| v2 (broken) | v3 (fixed) |
|---|---|
| `langfuse.trace(name=..., session_id=...)` | `langfuse.start_span(name=...)` + `.update_trace(session_id=...)` |
| `trace.generation(name=..., model=...)` | `trace.start_generation(name=..., model=...)` |
| `trace.span(name=..., input=...)` | `trace.start_span(name=..., input=...)` |
| `span.end(output={...})` | `span.update(output={...})` then `span.end()` |
